SELECT `p`.`appointment_id` AS `Id`, COALESCE((
    SELECT `d0`.`doctor_id`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p0`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d0` ON `p0`.`appointment_id` = `d0`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p0`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p0`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p0`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS `doctor`, (
    SELECT `d1`.`bed_id`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p1`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d1` ON `p1`.`appointment_id` = `d1`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p1`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p1`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p1`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `bed`, (
    SELECT `d2`.`nurse_assistant`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p2`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d2` ON `p2`.`appointment_id` = `d2`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p2`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p2`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p2`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `nurse`, (
    SELECT `d3`.`procedure`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p3`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d3` ON `p3`.`appointment_id` = `d3`.`appointment_id`

    LIMIT 1) AS `procedure`, (

    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p4`

    LIMIT 1) AS `status`
FROM `p_appointments` AS `p`
INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d` ON `p`.`appointment_id` = `d`.`appointment_id`

SELECT `h`.`employee_id`, (
    SELECT `h1`.`first_name`
    FROM `hr_employees` AS `h1`
    INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e0` ON `h1`.`employee_id` = `e0`.`employeeId`
    INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h2` ON `e0`.`employeeId` = `h2`.`employee_id`
    WHERE ((`e0`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e0`.`year` = @__year_1)) AND (`h`.`employee_id` = `h1`.`employee_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `firstName`, (
    SELECT `h3`.`middle_name`
    FROM `hr_employees` AS `h3`
    INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e1` ON `h3`.`employee_id` = `e1`.`employeeId`
    INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h4` ON `e1`.`employeeId` = `h4`.`employee_id`
    WHERE ((`e1`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e1`.`year` = @__year_1)) AND (`h`.`employee_id` = `h3`.`employee_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `middleName`, (
    SELECT `h5`.`last_name`
    FROM `hr_employees` AS `h5`
    INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e2` ON `h5`.`employee_id` = `e2`.`employeeId`
    INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h6` ON `e2`.`employeeId` = `h6`.`employee_id`
    WHERE ((`e2`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e2`.`year` = @__year_1)) AND (`h`.`employee_id` = `h5`.`employee_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `lastName`, (
    SELECT `h7`.`department`
    FROM `hr_employees` AS `h7`
    INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e3` ON `h7`.`employee_id` = `e3`.`employeeId`
    INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h8` ON `e3`.`employeeId` = `h8`.`employee_id`
    WHERE ((`e3`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e3`.`year` = @__year_1)) AND (`h`.`employee_id` = `h7`.`employee_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `department`, (
    SELECT `h9`.`role`
    FROM `hr_employees` AS `h9`
    INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e4` ON `h9`.`employee_id` = `e4`.`employeeId`
    INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h10` ON `e4`.`employeeId` = `h10`.`employee_id`
    WHERE ((`e4`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e4`.`year` = @__year_1)) AND (`h`.`employee_id` = `h9`.`employee_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `role`, (
    SELECT `h12`.`basic_pay`
    FROM `hr_employees` AS `h11`
    INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e5` ON `h11`.`employee_id` = `e5`.`employeeId`
    INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h12` ON `e5`.`employeeId` = `h12`.`employee_id`
    WHERE ((`e5`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e5`.`year` = @__year_1)) AND (`h`.`employee_id` = `h11`.`employee_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `basicSalary`, (
    SELECT `h14`.`overtime_pay`
    FROM `hr_employees` AS `h13`
    INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e6` ON `h13`.`employee_id` = `e6`.`employeeId`
    INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h14` ON `e6`.`employeeId` = `h14`.`employee_id`
    WHERE (((`e6`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e6`.`year` = @__year_1)) AND (`h`.`employee_id` = `h13`.`employee_id`)) AND (EXTRACT(day FROM `h14`.`pay_period_start`) = 16)
    LIMIT 1) AS `overtimePay`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN (`h0`.`employee_id` = `h`.`employee_id`) AND (EXTRACT(day FROM `h0`.`pay_period_start`) = 16) THEN `h0`.`sss_deduction`
END), 0.0) AS `deductions`, (
    SELECT `e7`.`monthTotalWage`
    FROM `hr_employees` AS `h15`
    INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e7` ON `h15`.`employee_id` = `e7`.`employeeId`
    INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h16` ON `e7`.`employeeId` = `h16`.`employee_id`
    WHERE (((`e7`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e7`.`year` = @__year_1)) AND (`h`.`employee_id` = `h15`.`employee_id`)) AND (((`e7`.`employeeId` = `h`.`employee_id`) AND (`e7`.`month` = @__month_0)) AND (`e7`.`year` = @__year_1))
    LIMIT 1) AS `netPay`
FROM `hr_employees` AS `h`
INNER JOIN `employeepayrollmonthreports` AS `e` ON `h`.`employee_id` = `e`.`employeeId`
INNER JOIN `hr_payroll` AS `h0` ON `e`.`employeeId` = `h0`.`employee_id`
WHERE (`e`.`month` = @__month_0) AND (`e`.`year` = @__year_1)
GROUP BY `h`.`employee_id`

2025-10-11 16:42:26.259 +08:00 [INF] Executed DbCommand (2ms) [Parameters=[@__month_0='?' (DbType = Int32), @__year_1='?' (DbType = Int32)], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT `p`.`appointment_id` AS `Id`, COALESCE((
    SELECT `d0`.`doctor_id`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p0`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d0` ON `p0`.`appointment_id` = `d0`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p0`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p0`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p0`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS `doctor`, (
    SELECT `d1`.`bed_id`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p1`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d1` ON `p1`.`appointment_id` = `d1`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p1`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p1`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p1`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `bed`, (
    SELECT `d2`.`nurse_assistant`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p2`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d2` ON `p2`.`appointment_id` = `d2`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p2`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p2`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p2`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `nurse`, (
    SELECT `d3`.`procedure`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p3`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d3` ON `p3`.`appointment_id` = `d3`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p3`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p3`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p3`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `procedure`, (
    SELECT `d4`.`status`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p4`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d4` ON `p4`.`appointment_id` = `d4`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p4`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p4`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p4`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `status`
FROM `p_appointments` AS `p`
INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d` ON `p`.`appointment_id` = `d`.`appointment_id`
WHERE (EXTRACT(month FROM `p`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)
GROUP BY `p`.`appointment_id`
